# Detector benchmark summary (fixed subsample of each split)

Splits: train (4000), test (400), test_ood (400), test_fr (400), test_fiction (200), test_attack (400).
Intervals: 95% bootstrap, 1000 resamples, stratified by label, seed 20261112.
TPR and FPR in %. `TPR@1%` uses the threshold of the same split; `xfer` columns use the threshold fixed on `test`. `FPR got` is the false-positive rate actually reached at the same-split 1% threshold (ties can push it below 1%).
